"apa kabar tuan?" literally translates to "how are you, sir?"	əpaka bəɾtuan
"kabar baik" literally means "good news" but on a number of occasion can also mean "i'm well" (in response to "how are you?")	kɑbɛrbæjk
"skak" is "check" in chess	skakˀ
